92:    private void request(String reason){
93:        if(!enabled || !"OFF".equals(mode) || !running.compareAndSet(false,true))return;
94:        try { executor.execute(()-> { try { runPass(reason); } finally { running.set(false); } }); }
95:        catch(RuntimeException rejected){running.set(false);log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_REJECTED] reason={}",reason,rejected);}
96:    }
97:
98:    private void runPass(String reason){
99:        if(drain!=null&&!drain.beginReconciliation())return;
100:        try {
101:            // The server-time read happens before every REST kline request and is
102:            // the sole exchange-time reference for this pass.
103:            Instant exchangeNow=binance.getServerTime();
104:            Instant databaseNow=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)",Timestamp.class).toInstant();
105:            int[] remaining={Math.max(0,requestBudget)};
106:            for(Candidate candidate:openCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
107:                if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
108:                if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
109:                recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,false);
110:            }
111:            if(remaining[0]>0) for(Candidate candidate:gapCandidates(exchangeNow,databaseNow)) {
112:                if(drain!=null&&drain.draining())return;
113:                if(remaining[0]--<=0)break;
114:                recover(candidate,exchangeNow,databaseNow,true);
115:            }
116:            log.debug("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS] reason={}, exchangeNow={}, budget={}",reason,exchangeNow,requestBudget-remaining[0]);
117:        } catch(RuntimeException failure) {
118:            log.warn("[FIX-153][RECONCILIATION_PASS_FAILED] reason={}; no candle was treated as recovered",reason,failure);
119:        } finally {if(drain!=null)drain.completeReconciliation();}
120:    }
121:
122:    private List<Candidate> openCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
123:        List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
124:        for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
125:            long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
126:            Instant lower=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window);
127:            Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
128:            Instant latestOpen=eligible.minusSeconds(seconds);
129:            result.addAll(jdbc.query("""
130:                SELECT symbol,interval_code,open_time FROM candle
131:                WHERE closed=0 AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<=?
132:                  AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL')
133:                ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 50
134:                """,(rs,n)->{
135:                    Instant open=rs.getTimestamp(3).toInstant();
136:                    return new Candidate(rs.getString(1),rs.getString(2),open,expectedClose(open,rs.getString(2)));
137:                },interval,Timestamp.from(lower),Timestamp.from(latestOpen)));
138:        }
139:        return result.stream().filter(c->audit.due(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,databaseNow))
140:            .filter(this::positiveLossEvidence).toList();
141:    }
142:
143:    /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
144:    private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
145:        List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
146:        for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
147:            long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
148:            Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
149:            Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
150:            Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
151:                SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
152:                """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
153:            for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
154:                if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
155:                    result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
156:            }
157:        }
158:        return result;
159:    }
160:
161:    private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
162:        var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
163:        int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
164:        try {
165:            List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
166:            List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
167:            if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
168:                List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
169:                if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
170:                    terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
171:                }
172:                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
173:            }
174:            if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
175:            if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
176:                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
177:            }
178:            if(exact.size()!=1) {
179:                pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
180:            }
181:            BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
182:            tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
183:                boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
184:                    k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
185:                    : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
186:                    k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
187:                String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
188:                audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
189:                if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
190:            });
191:        } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
192:    }
193:
194:    private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
195:        long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
196:        audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
197:    }
198:    private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
199:        // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
200:        // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
201:        // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
202:        // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
203:        Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
204:        Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
205:            candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
206:        return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
207:    }
208:    private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
209:        tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
210:            audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
211:            outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
212:        });
213:    }
214:    private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
215:    private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
216:    private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
217:}

322:# MANDATORY PRE-IMPLEMENTATION CHANGE CONTRACT
323:
324:This section extends the existing governance. It does not replace any earlier
325:project, investigation, architecture, trading, safety, or documentation rule.
326:
327:For any material code change, implementation is forbidden until the team has
328:produced and approved the same exact revision of:
329:
330:`agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`
331:
332:The specification is the implementation contract and MUST contain, at minimum:
333:
334:1. Business objective and the exact agreed business line/flow.
335:2. Confirmed root cause and evidence.
336:3. In-scope and out-of-scope boundaries.
337:4. Complete class/file change registry (modify/create/delete/rename/config/
338:   migration/test/documentation).
339:5. Class-by-class and material method-by-method current behavior -> agreed new
340:   behavior, reason, inputs/outputs, side effects, error handling, concurrency,
341:   transactionality, Replay impact and Production impact.
342:6. Mandatory logging registry covering all materially affected areas. Logs must
343:   make the business flow and decisions reconstructable without exposing
344:   credentials/secrets. Include relevant correlation/trade/symbol/interval/
345:   candle/test identifiers and respect the project's UTC/KSA rules.
346:7. Database, configuration and trading-behavior registries, explicitly stating
347:   NONE when there is no impact.
348:8. Test and validation registry, including positive, negative, regression,
349:   failure/recovery, concurrency where relevant, Replay, and Production-safe
350:   validation.
351:9. Risks, rollback plan, observability plan and documentation registry.
352:10. Master implementation registry assigning an ID to every authorized change,
353:    log, DB/config item, test and documentation update.
354:
355:No source/config/database/trading change may be implemented unless it is
356:registered in the approved specification. No unrelated refactoring or cleanup
357:may be hidden inside an approved fix.

1013:        return "Admin Profit Lock: activation=" + nvl(s.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70)).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()
1014:                + "%, initial=" + nvl(s.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()

1018:    private BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPercent(ShadowPosition position, BigDecimal remainingLegPnl) {
1019:        BigDecimal totalPnl = position.partialRealizedPnl().add(remainingLegPnl, MC);
1020:        BigDecimal totalInvestedCost = position.cost().add(position.partialHarvestCostBasis(), MC);
1021:        return totalInvestedCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
1022:                : totalPnl.divide(totalInvestedCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
1023:    }
1024:
1025:    private BigDecimal percentage(BigDecimal entry,BigDecimal exit){return exit.subtract(entry).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).divide(entry,8,RoundingMode.HALF_UP);}

1027:    private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d){return d==SignalDecision.SELL||d==SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;}

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/service/CandleDataQualityService.java:1-120
1:package com.crypto.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.config.AnalysisScoringProperties;
4:import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
5:import com.crypto.dto.CandleDataQualityResult;
6:import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
7:import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
8:import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
9:import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
10:import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
11:
12:import java.time.Duration;
13:import java.util.ArrayList;
14:import java.util.Comparator;
15:import java.util.List;
16:
17:@Service
18:@RequiredArgsConstructor
19:public class CandleDataQualityService {
20:
21:    private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
22:    private final AnalysisScoringProperties properties;
23:
24:    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
25:    public CandleDataQualityResult validate(String symbol, String intervalCode) {
26:        int minimum = properties.dataQuality().minimumCandles();
27:        List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(
28:                symbol, intervalCode, PageRequest.of(0, minimum)
29:        ));
30:        candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));
31:
32:        List<String> warnings = new ArrayList<>();
33:        int invalid = 0;
34:        for (Candle candle : candles) {
35:            boolean invalidOhlc = candle.getOpenPrice() == null
36:                    || candle.getHighPrice() == null
37:                    || candle.getLowPrice() == null
38:                    || candle.getClosePrice() == null
39:                    || candle.getVolume() == null
40:                    || candle.getOpenPrice().signum() <= 0
41:                    || candle.getHighPrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
42:                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getLowPrice()) < 0
43:                    || candle.getClosePrice().compareTo(candle.getHighPrice()) > 0
44:                    || candle.getVolume().signum() < 0;
45:            if (invalidOhlc) {
46:                invalid++;
47:            }
48:        }
49:
50:        Duration expectedStep = intervalDuration(intervalCode);
51:        int missing = 0;
52:        if (!expectedStep.isZero()) {
53:            for (int index = 1; index < candles.size(); index++) {
54:                Duration actual = Duration.between(candles.get(index - 1).getOpenTime(), candles.get(index).getOpenTime());
55:                if (actual.compareTo(expectedStep) > 0) {
56:                    long steps = actual.toMillis() / expectedStep.toMillis();
57:                    missing += Math.max(0, (int) steps - 1);
58:                }
59:            }
60:        } else {
61:            warnings.add("Unsupported interval for gap validation: " + intervalCode);
62:        }
63:
64:        if (candles.size() < minimum) {
65:            warnings.add("Insufficient history: " + candles.size() + "/" + minimum + " closed candles");
66:        }
67:        if (missing > 0) {
68:            warnings.add("Missing " + missing + " candle(s) in the analysis window");
69:        }
70:        if (invalid > 0) {
71:            warnings.add("Found " + invalid + " candle(s) with invalid OHLCV data");
72:        }
73:
74:        boolean valid = candles.size() >= minimum
75:                && missing <= properties.dataQuality().maximumMissingCandles()
76:                && (!properties.dataQuality().blockOnInvalidOhlc() || invalid == 0);
77:
78:        return new CandleDataQualityResult(valid, minimum, candles.size(), missing, invalid, List.copyOf(warnings));
79:    }
80:
81:    private Duration intervalDuration(String intervalCode) {
82:        if (intervalCode == null || intervalCode.length() < 2) {
83:            return Duration.ZERO;
84:        }
85:        try {
86:            long amount = Long.parseLong(intervalCode.substring(0, intervalCode.length() - 1));
87:            char unit = intervalCode.charAt(intervalCode.length() - 1);
88:            return switch (unit) {
89:                case 'm' -> Duration.ofMinutes(amount);
90:                case 'h' -> Duration.ofHours(amount);
91:                case 'd' -> Duration.ofDays(amount);
92:                case 'w' -> Duration.ofDays(amount * 7);
93:                default -> Duration.ZERO;
94:            };
95:        } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
96:            return Duration.ZERO;
97:        }
98:    }
99:}

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.java:1-90
1:package com.crypto.wallet.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
4:import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
5:import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
6:import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
7:
8:import java.math.BigDecimal;
9:import java.math.MathContext;
10:import java.util.Locale;
11:
12:/**
13: * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
14: *
15: * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
16: * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
17: * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
18: * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
19: *
20: * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
21: * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
22: */
23:@Component
24:@RequiredArgsConstructor
25:@Slf4j
26:public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
27:    private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
28:
29:    private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
30:    public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
31:        if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
32:                || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
33:            return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
34:        }
35:        String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
36:        BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
37:        BigDecimal minimum;
38:        try {
39:            minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
40:        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
41:            // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
42:            // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
43:            log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
44:                    pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
45:            return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
46:        }
47:        if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
48:            return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
49:        }
50:        return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
51:    }
52:
53:    public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
54:    }
55:}

7:import java.util.Set;

9:/** FIX-125: fail before a shared-wallet write/locking read if the caller skipped
10: * the global guard. This wraps Spring Data interfaces, including inherited save
11: * methods. It is not authorization for arbitrary raw SQL or EntityManager use;
12: * the named source-inventory test prohibits adding such wallet writers silently. */
13:@Component
14:public class WalletMutationRepositoryGuard implements BeanPostProcessor {
15:    private static final Set<String> GUARDED=Set.of("walletAssetRepository","walletDailyStatisticsRepository",
16:        "walletSettingsRepository","walletTradeRepository","walletCashFlowRepository","walletSnapshotRepository");
17:    public static void check(String repository,String method) {
18:        if(GUARDED.contains(repository) && (method.startsWith("save") || method.startsWith("delete") || method.equals("flush")
19:            || method.contains("ForUpdate") || method.equals("creditQuantity") || method.equals("debitQuantityIfSufficient")))
20:            WalletTransactionCoordination.requireMutation();
21:    }
22:    @Override public Object postProcessAfterInitialization(Object bean,String name) {
23:        if(!GUARDED.contains(name))return bean;
24:        ProxyFactory proxy=new ProxyFactory(bean);
25:        proxy.addAdvice((MethodInterceptor) call->{
26:            check(name,call.getMethod().getName());
27:            return call.proceed();
28:        });
29:        return proxy.getProxy();
30:    }
31:}

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:1-260
1:package com.crypto.regression.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
4:import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
5:
6:import java.util.ArrayList;
7:import java.util.List;
8:import java.util.Objects;
9:
10:/**
11: * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
12: * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
13: */
14:@Service
15:public class ReplayParityValidator {
16:
17:    public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
18:        List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
19:        if (production == null || replay == null) {
20:            mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
21:            return new Result(false, mismatches);
22:        }
23:
24:        same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
25:        same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
26:        same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
27:        same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
28:        same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
29:        same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
30:        same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
31:        same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
32:        same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
33:        same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
34:        same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
35:        same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
36:        same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
37:                production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
38:        return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
39:    }
40:
41:    private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
42:        if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
43:            mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
44:        }
45:    }
46:
47:    public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
48:}

--- src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayDataset.java:1-160
1:package com.crypto.regression.service;
2:
3:import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
4:
5:import java.time.Instant;
6:import java.util.ArrayList;
7:import java.util.HashMap;
8:import java.util.List;
9:import java.util.Map;
10:
11:/**
12: * FIX-11H REPLAY/PARITY INFRASTRUCTURE ONLY.
13: *
14: * Golden Rule: Replay = Production business logic. This immutable run-local dataset changes
15: * only how historical candle/lineage inputs are obtained. It never changes their meaning,
16: * filtering, ordering, limits, indicator math, thresholds, or execution rules.
17: */
18:public final class ReplayDataset {
19:    public record CandleKey(String symbol, String interval) {}
20:    public record SignalKey(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleOpenTime) {}
21:
22:    private final Map<CandleKey, List<Candle>> candlesByKey;
23:    private final Map<SignalKey, Long> lineage;
24:
25:    ReplayDataset(Map<CandleKey, List<Candle>> candlesByKey, Map<SignalKey, Long> lineage) {
26:        Map<CandleKey, List<Candle>> copy = new HashMap<>();
27:        candlesByKey.forEach((key, value) -> copy.put(key, List.copyOf(value)));
28:        this.candlesByKey = Map.copyOf(copy);
29:        this.lineage = Map.copyOf(lineage);
30:    }
31:
32:    /** Exact cached equivalent of findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore: <= boundary, DESC, LIMIT. */
33:    public List<Candle> closedCandlesAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant maxOpenTime, int limit) {
34:        List<Candle> ascending = candlesByKey.getOrDefault(new CandleKey(symbol, interval), List.of());
35:        int low = 0, high = ascending.size();
36:        while (low < high) {
37:            int mid = (low + high) >>> 1;
38:            if (!ascending.get(mid).getOpenTime().isAfter(maxOpenTime)) low = mid + 1;
39:            else high = mid;
40:        }
41:        int endExclusive = low;
42:        int startInclusive = Math.max(0, endExclusive - Math.max(0, limit));
43:        List<Candle> result = new ArrayList<>(endExclusive - startInclusive);
44:        for (int i = endExclusive - 1; i >= startInclusive; i--) result.add(ascending.get(i));
45:        return result;
46:    }
47:
48:    /** FIX-112D exact identity only. No nearest-time fallback. */
49:    public Long sourceSignalId(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleOpenTime) {
50:        return lineage.get(new SignalKey(symbol, interval, candleOpenTime));
51:    }
52:}

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.6 is not the canonical approval contract required by governance. Governance names `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md`, which remains RPR-0.4; RPR-0.6 says it supersedes earlier revisions “for review only.” Its approval therefore cannot authorize the specified design process.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The “complete” registry is incorrect. It names nonexistent `regression_test_run` rather than `analysis_test_run`, and omits `RegressionTestService`, which creates runs and owns cancellation, purge, archive, and ID-reset behavior.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** M02 does not prevent historical look-ahead. It only requires a candle be `closed=1`; it does not require that the candle was closed when Production made the decision. Current reconciliation can later close an open row or add a closed backfill. The `Candle` projection does not expose `created_at`, `updated_at`, provenance, or reconciliation resolution time needed to establish historical visibility.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The present Replay timeline is not even restricted to closed candles: it loads all candles by identity. Snapshot queries then apply the row’s current `closed=1` state. Production data quality likewise evaluates currently closed rows. The proposed qualifier must define and enforce one decision-time finality rule across every Replay and shared candle read.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Isolation is specified as an outcome, not an enforceable design. Current Replay reads `wallet_settings`; minimum-notional evaluation may call Binance HTTP; the existing wallet guard only protects writes and locking reads. RPR-0.6 does not define dependency wiring, propagation across async work, or a complete interception boundary.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** RPR-0.6 requires retained price-event ID/order data, but the current `PriceEvent` query orders by `id` while discarding it from the returned record. `MarketPriceEventService`/`PriceEvent` are absent from the change registry, so the required behavior is unregistered.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** M04’s staging requirement is not implementable as specified. Current Replay writes output incrementally during processing. The artifact does not define staging table names, promotion transaction, cancellation/restart/purge behavior, or treatment of independently committed diagnostics/audits.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The artifact conflicts with the requested “Production code unchanged” boundary: it authorizes modifications to shared Production services, while omitting existing shared dependencies such as `ExecutionIntelligenceService` and `ExecutionPriceAuthorityService`. It must explicitly choose Replay-local adapters only, or authorize narrowly defined shared refactors with equivalence proof.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `ANALYSIS_PARITY` is not presently grounded in a version/config identity source. The artifact acknowledges missing deployed JAR/config lineage but defines no evidence source or refusal algorithm that makes this claim safely attainable.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** These are material design omissions, not implementation details. The specification is not complete or safe for dual-agent technical-design approval.

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Define concrete staging schemas, atomic promotion, diagnostics, crash/restart, cancellation, archive, and purge semantics.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Register every required source change, including run lifecycle, price-event projection/ordering, all shared dependencies, and isolation ports/guards.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Resolve whether shared Production source changes are allowed. If yes, specify each method’s unchanged Production behavior and exact regression proof.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Define the historical code/config identity source required for `ANALYSIS_PARITY`, or remove that label from Release 1.
